# Recorded results of the Lean model K0Part.step for the host test in
# model_tests.rs.  GENERATED: the hashes are written by `lake exe vectors`
# in proofs/lean, never by hand.  proofs/lean/README.md says how to check
# the file against the model and what to do after a deliberate change of
# Core or of the model.
#
# One script per line, fields separated by ';':
#   id ; boot tick ; domain of task 0, 1, ... ; first task ; ready tasks ;
#   waiting tasks as tid@deadline or tid@- ; events ; hash
# Events: tN tick, pN poll, y yield, b- and bN block, wT wake, xT wakeX,
# sD:W,D:W,... set_table.
# The hash is the model's result: FNV-1a 64 over the canonical lines of the
# script (the boot state, then the state after every event), every line
# followed by a newline.  proofs/lean/ModelVectors.lean defines the line and
# the hash.
#
# The scripts are random scripts of the differential harness that compared
# Core<u64> with K0Part.step (the harness is not in the repository): seeds
# 20261115 to 20261120, one for each kind below, every script cut to
# 16, 24, 32, 48 or 80 events, none with a deadline 0.
# 941 of the 1600 were chosen to raise the count of every path of
# Core::tick, poll, yield_now, block and set_table and of both wake events
# (the goal was 300, and 120 for the paths that only kind i reaches);
# the others were taken in turn from the kinds m, k, p, t, e.  The test
# counts the paths and fails below 100 of each.
# The letter of an id is the kind of script:
#   m  any order of events, tick indexes never go back (422 scripts)
#   k  only the call patterns of the kernel (325 scripts)
#   p  as k, and poll while a task runs (222 scripts)
#   t  as k, and tick while nothing runs, repeated tick indexes (252 scripts)
#   e  as k, and set_table while nothing runs, tables without the running domain (209 scripts)
#   i  as m, and yield, block and wakes while nothing runs (170 scripts)
# The kernel raises none of the events that kind i adds.  They are here
# because nothing else reaches the early return of Core::yield_now.  A block
# while nothing runs is the one event the two sides treat differently: Core
# looks into the queue, which is empty then, and the model does nothing.
